Quote: Maniac, 2004-10-22T19:20:19Z
There's this one steak at school who's really annoying, yet is madly in love with me. I've tried everything I can think of to pry her away, but to no avail. What's a legal, non-swearing action that can get here to stop loving me?
Well, son, it depends on the kind of steak she is. If she's really irritating and unpleasant, you need to talk badly about her to her friends who you know will tell her what you said. Just bring up that she's really been annoying you lately, and then start naming things that piss you off about her. Make stuff up, even. Don't make it too extreme like "I'm tempted to remove her hideous visage with an ice pick," but rather along the lines of "she does this squinty thing all the damn time when she's trying to look cute, and it really makes her look quite hideous." Say things that aren't serious enough to be told on for, but serious enough to give her the message that you don't like her and don't want to be associated with her.
If she's damn fine, then you, my friend, should go drink liquid tile cleaner. Why the hell would you not accept some good ol' fashioned good times with someone like that?
If you like this steak, too, pretend you don't care about her at all, and she'll go nutso for ya. I'm dead serious, it doesn't look like it'll work, but it works wonders, even when you think it isn't.

Quote: 654321, 2004-10-23T02:07:55Z
This kid in my art class really pisses me off. All he does is try to get me into detention. He has not succeeded, but if he continues, my teacher will say that we'll both get detention and a phone home. What the hell should I do?
Ignore him completely. When he does anything to you, don't react at all, and then come up to the teacher and tell him/her what the other kid is doing. That's right, I want you to tattle. Any other course of action will land you in detention, and I'm assuming that's what you want to avoid...
If he gets mad and starts threatening you, go right back to the teacher. A lot of kids are funny in that they keep threatening you even though you're feeding it all directly to the teacher. If he threatens you online, save the conversation. Make sure to have witnesses if it's in person. If it's through email, forward it to the teacher.
Teachers do funny things in these situations, when they have a constant stream of the bad actions of one particular student.
But most importantly, ask other kids to complain about him, too. If it's more than one voice against just him, in the teacher's mind, there's something horribly wrong with that one kid.
Just let the teacher do the work for you.

How many licks does it take to get to the center of a tootsie pop?
Depends on the flavor of the hard candy. Each individual flavor has different substances in it to make it that color and that taste, and so depending upon the color, the candy might be harder/more dense and thus more resistant to a lick. It is my firm belief that chocolate takes 312 licks, at most, before you bite (so it'll never be known how many licks it truly takes), blue takes 3214 licks, though it's an arduous task, red can only be licked a few times before bitten, and no one in their right mind would touch an orange with their tongue.

Alright, Kablizzy, I'm going to review what you've said about it in detail... assuming I can find the post...

Your idea so far of trying to find a higher-paying job and moving away would work pretty well, but there are a few things you'd have to consider.
If you're a main source of income for your mother and grandmother, it'd be harder on them if you moved away. It seems like they don't just need your money, but your prescence as well. My suggestion? This is based on very little, mind you. You might want to consider putting your grandmother into a retirement home. I don't entirely know your financial situation, but... you and your mother provide for the three of you. With your grandmother out of the financial picture, your mother would be able to take care of herself, right? You right now can already provide for yourself, especially if you have a working fiancee. The main thing you'd have to consider is how much it would take keeping your grandmother in the home. The combined efforts of you, your mother, and your fiancee (only if she is willing), and anything your grandmother may collect from being retired should cover that. But then again that's with an assumption of your financial situation. It may be very hard to put grandma in a home, but you'd still be able to visit her, talk to her, call her, etc., and it's a problem you'll have to face eventually (you don't want to be stuck in a rut simply for convenience of seeing your grandmother), and it's better sooner than later. Man, this probably sounds heartless, and I really am not meaning it to.
Hopefully, this will leave your mother, your grandmother, and yourself more or less separate entities as opposed to all relying on each other. From there, you might want to look into jobs in your field. I don't know what you're interested in, but if you're looking for something along the lines of where you work, you have plenty of experience and a godsend of positive referrals from your co-workers.
Anyway, look for that job in some far-away place, and when you find it, give your current employer a warning that you'll be moving away for family reasons, and that they should seriously look into hiring some more people (from what I gather, you're their saving grace).
The last bit is saving up money for the move. Hopefully the home idea will put you with a nice bit of profit, and you can save that up to move away and establish your life elsewhere. Just remember to always plan as much as you can. When you find your job, think of how you're going to get to it, where you're going to live, etc. Time spent considering this will also give your employer time to find some new people.
You'll have to leave the life you now know partially in the dust. This means leaving behind your mother and grandmother, though you're still perfectly able to send checks and call home as often as you like, or even visit for holidays if you're not that far away.
As for her mother, in the midst of all this, act like absolutely nothing is going on. You've been seeing your fiancee secretly... does her mother even remember who you are? Well, in either case, once you have the money and everything set up, just leave and don't turn back, breaking off all communication with her mother. Change cell phones (you'll have to anyway for the new area), don't give out new home numbers, places of work, etc. Tell your fiancee to not return her calls, or even speak with her for that matter. If this woman is really as dangerous as you say she is, your fiancee shouldn't really have trouble with it. But then again, I don't entirely know.

This is assuming you want to do it the passive, secretive way. The other method is to stand up to her mother, pile up some evidence, and let her mother incriminate herself. One of the better ways to do it is to send her a letter. This will prompt her to send a letter back. Just imagine what this letter will be filled with.
Use this as well as your and your fiancee's testimonies to get some restraining order action on her. From this point, whether she ends up in jail or with a restraining order, you should stop worrying about it, but still watch your back. If she leaves you alone for the rest of your life, good for you. If she tries something stupid, all the worse for her. Make it painful for her to continue to persue your relationship with her daughter. Make it arduous and riddled along the way with physical, emotional, and legal obstacles. Something I'd personally do, but only because I'm sneaky like that, is to give her other problems to worry about in the meanwhile. Make it so that she is forced to focus on other things as well, which will distract her and kill her morale to work against you.

Whatever you decide to do, it's very important that you act now. I'm very serious, act right now. After reading this post, and maybe posting some other stuff, start looking for a job far away, and start thinking of a way to tell your mother that you believe it's better for everyone if your grandmother were to be put in a home. Waiting only makes the whole deal exponentially harder.

Hell, if you're in my area or moving into my area, I'd be glad to help out with things on a personal or physical level. You want me to help you move? You want to talk to me in person? I'll do the best I can.
I live in central California, in what would be considered the San Francisco area on a large scale. I'm within a few hundred miles of San Francisco.

And if you find later in life that your fiancee's mother reappears at an unexpected time, then she's really in deep shit. If you didn't tell her where you are, those are some serious stalking charges, and her anger at you will probably make her a lawsuit factory. Other than that, she'll be violating restraining order charges, or she broke out of prison (???). You can overpower an old lady, can't you? If worst comes to worst, you may very well have to, but only defensively. It also doesn't sound like your mother-in-law will have friends to harass you for her.

I'm the kind of guy who faces his problems head-on, and no matter how overwhelming, I bulldoze my way through them. Charge forward with full fury, and never let up. Never. I would choose the second method of getting her out of your life as opposed to you out of hers.

This will obviously be an evolving issue. If you wouldn't mind, I'd like to stay informed. Feel free to PM me, or if you feel secure enough about it, just keep on posting about it.

Quote: Maniac, 2004-10-26T00:29:35Z
In your opinion, why do you think W&W exists?
In this world, Maniac, we have a few different kinds of people. We have smart people. Smart people tend to know more about the world, can adapt to it more easily, and are usually less angry for no reason and use a degree of sophistication when addressing their surroundings. We have people in the middle (or, "children"). These people are pretty damn stupid, but they have an excuse: they're just kids. Fortunately, some of the "children" are included in the "smart people" category. Then we have stupid people. These people, try as they might, cannot attain a sentience more complex than an average culinary (or, for a closer comparison, a goldfish). These people are the main populus of internet forums. Then we have a sack of hammers. It's not that bright, but it can kind of be useful sometimes when you want to use a big, dead weight. And lastly, we have people like -HC-Capt and Wicked&Weird. Entirely worthless, and once (1200's CE) thought to be demons, these people are barely considered human. They eat (just about anything), sleep (when they're not eating), and defacate (on your fresh laundry, their food dish, and online forums).
We need this balance of people in our world, Maniac. It's very important for you to understand this. Without stupid people and children, who would we insult to make ourselves feel better?

Quote: Maniac, 2004-10-26T00:29:35Z
Please explain why people become idiots when they sit down in front of a computer.
When a person sits down at a computer, the hypnotic effects of the fancy box of colored lights carry out their intended and lethal purpose: to make the population of Earth stupid. It's all part of Bill Gates' master plan. Have you ever seen that guy use a computer besides the one he made Windows on? Did you know he's completely computer illiterate? It's because he doesn't want to take the chance of becoming a moron.
Too many people become gibbering morons when they use a computer. These are the people with no willpower, and are doomed to a life of stupidity (like our good friend -HC-Capt).
And then there are the crusaders. The crusaders are holy and determined warriors sent from the uppermost levels of Heaven to lead the masses of slowly more stupified peoples away from the foul temptations of the Great Satan (Bill Gates; people usually call him "The Great Stan," however). Crusaders can be found using proper mechanics and stating perceptive and influential posts on forums and at least attempting to spell things right (spelling is actually a ritual we stole from Pagans back in the 18th century, and so angels have a hard time using it as the only means of communication with their misguided mortal inferiors.

Quote: Brickman, 2004-10-26T01:02:17Z
What does the BBQ in OMGWTFBBQ mean?
Well, Brickman, OMGWTFBBQ stands quite clearly for: nothing! It doesn't mean a damn thing and was put in by the guy who invented and maintains the internet on his home computer to confuse poor souls like you. These days, it's used as a throwaway phrase, like the American "like" or the Spanish "che."
For example, you could be talking to your friend in person, and he says something like this:
"Okay, so like I walked to the movie theater and tried to like buy a ticket. I ask like the guy there what the price is, and he's like, 'for you, honey, much lower... like my standards.'"
But if he were to relate this to you in the form of a text message, it would look something like this:
"k so liek i walkd 2 the food network n ask 2 by a tic n the guy ther was liek OMGWTFBBQ"
Make another post if that doesn't make things clearer, but get a micro wave first, because I want to see the expression on your face before I delete it.

Smart people tend to know more about the world, can adapt to it more easily, and are usually less angry for no reason and use a degree of sophistication when addressing their surroundings. We have people in the middle (or, "children"). These people are pretty damn stupid, but they have an excuse: they're just kids. Fortunately, some of the "children" are included in the "smart people" category. Then we have stupid people. These people, try as they might, cannot attain a sentience more complex than an average culinary (or, for a closer comparison, a goldfish). These people are the main populus of internet forums. Then we have a sack of hammers. It's not that bright, but it can kind of be useful sometimes when you want to use a big, dead weight. And lastly, we have people like -HC-Capt and Wicked&Weird. Entirely worthless, and once (1200's CE) thought to be demons, these people are barely considered human. They eat (just about anything), sleep (when they're not eating), and defacate (on your fresh laundry, their food dish, and online forums).
We need this balance of people in our world, Maniac. It's very important for you to understand this. Without stupid people and children, who would we insult to make ourselves feel better?

I wandered into the computer lab one day to find someone playing N. He was playing level 3-0, and was kinda doing poorly. When I asked him where he got it, he referred me to www.gamehippo.com. I've only used that site to get N, mind you, because most of the other games are mind-numbingly boring.
N was by far the coolest thing I had seen in a game, so I started playing it a whole lot. I beat the game in a few hours (this was back in the days of v1.2, with only 10 episodes). I, not knowing there was such a thing (shut up, I didn't read the manual ), immediately regained interest. After reading the manual, I started looking for a database of levels somewhere online. Googling "N" was one of the stupidest things I've tried, so I eventually tracked down the main website of metanet, and saw that there was a forum, with a Custom Levels section, which explains why I went there exclusively for so long. I didn't even bother to check any other forum.
I stay in this forum because the main posters are actually quite intelligent, and I can learn something from them. Haha, no, I'm kidding, I'm too brilliant for that as it is. Ah, anyway...
Plenty of people are fun to talk with, and idiots are easy to spot and avoid. Plus, it's also kinda fun playing with idiots and insulting them. It's like one of those little clown punching bag things, except that they don't laugh when you hit them.

By now, I've lost plenty of interest in N, but my interest was rekindled again with the promise of v1.4. Today, I stick around because of the fun in discussions we have in non-N-related forums. Plus, I'm a moderator, and how cool is that?

#36771 51 2004-11-18T08:10:30Z LittleViking
This could start a small digital copyright debate I guess.

Some groups are now urging the Supreme Court to look at the betamax decision (that made VCRs legal) and possibly overrule it. These groups see file sharing programs (like Kazaa, Limewire, Bearshare, and others if anyone doesn't know), widely used to infringe music copyrights, growing out of control and think they can stop them by eliminating "fair use" as defined by the decision. Fair use includes such things as making one VHS copy of a movie you bought or making a backup copy of a software CD. In an extreme reading, even listening to a friend's music CD that you yourself didn't pay for could be seen as illegal.


So my question is, what do you think about the whole file-sharing topic?

#38885 57 2004-12-01T08:13:44Z Tsukatu
Quote: Bananakid, 2004-11-30T05:10:15Z
My male parental unit has installed a nasty bit of net-cencor on my computer, because he's a twat. I've already stopped it from doing the email reports, timing my activity, and choosing online hours. However, it's capabilities to stop me from allowing Trillian (my IM client) to connect to the internet, and it also stops certain websites from showing, replacing it with thus. I have removed all traces from my hard drive and startup list, yes is inists on being an asshat. Any advice?

What is the software called? I may have heard of it.
Ookay, if you really get desperate, I'd suggest playing around with your registry editor.
Go to Start -> Run, and type in "regedit" and press enter. Ok, now lemme guess, the thing isn't giving you priveleges to access the registry editor, right? If that happens, go to Start -> Run again, and type in:
REG add HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion\Policies\System /v DisableRegistryTools /t REG_DWORD /d 0 /f
...and press enter.
Ok, then find the software thing in the registry, probably somewhere in HKCU\Software or HKCU\Software\Microsoft\Windows\CurrentVersion or something, depending on how big of an impact this thing has on your system. Either way, find it and start fucking around with its permissions. Find random bits and change values. When my mom did that and I changed lots of string values to "Fuck fuck shit, drink your milk!" she stopped reinstalling it, and finally I did something (I don't even know, I was just replacing lots of important-looking stuff) to make the program crash every time I started Windows, but thanks to a Microsoft error, it still looked like the program was running....
Anyway, you know how your mommy and daddy have always told you never to fuck around with the Registry Editor? Ignore that. It's my playground, anyway.

Quote: nevermore, 2005-06-19T06:19:46Z
Can you explain the details of satanism? I don't understand... If you don't believe in religion, how can you believe in an all-powerful demon? Technically, it is a religion. In fact, if you believe Satan is your god, then you're a theist.
Yes yes, Mr. I'm-above-actually-finding-out-for-myself-what-Satanism-is-so-I-think-I'll-make-a-typical-assumption-about-Satanism-even -though-it's-quite-clearly-untrue-based-on-what-I've-seen-from-Tsukatu's-blatant-antitheistic-beliefs.

I'm going to give you the shortest summary of Satanism I can think of:
God sucks. Be free.

Okay, now I'm going to give you a slightly longer summary.

Atheism is the denial of Christianity.
Antitheism is the direct opposition to Christianity.
Whereas an atheist would say, "God does not exist," an antitheist would say, "God does not exist, and you're a dumbass if you think He does," and then add some ramblings about the negative influence theism has had on the world and individual.
Humanism is a philosophy... like a way to live your life. It's a lifestyle. A humanist indulges himself in pleasures of the flesh. He believes in the power of the individual. More on this a little later.
Satanism = Antitheism particularly geared against Christianity + Humanism + a pinch of religious fervor.

Main points:
-To a Satanist, Christianity is absolutely the dumbest thing anyone could possibly hope to invent or follow. It's absurd from its roots to its present condition, and anyone who follows is, simply put, scum. It's not a class issue, it's an intellectual issue.
-There ain't no Heaven, and there ain't no Hell, so have your fun while you're alive.
-The phrase "indulge on pleasures of the flesh" sounds grossly misleading, of course, so here's a better way of putting it: Do what makes you happy, as long as your pleasure doesn't cause anyone any excessive amount of direct or indirect harm. For most people, making other people happy makes them happy, so Satanists are typically among the more generous and friendly. Most people have more fun with friends, and have fun making friends, so Satanists are encouraged to be polite and respectful.
-Remember what Jesus said? "If someone strike thee on one cheek, turn the other like a dumbass?" Fuck Jesus, try "if someone strikes thee on one cheek, smash him on the other." Satanism uses its own version of the Golden Rule: "At the first encounter, treat others how you want to be treated. From that point on, treat them how they treat you." If you start out friendly and they respond with friendliness (i.e. you're both Satanists), then you're better off. But if you start out friendly and they act like a dick, then you shouldn't waste your time with them, and you've successfully avoided a stupid problem that'd only grow if you would've remained nice like a dumbass Christian.
-Christianity preaches abstinence, promising a better life after death. Isn't that just retarded though? Ignoring your natural urges only leads to your discomfort and your frustration, turning you into a prudish nazi homophobe racist. You should follow through with your uges (provided they don't harm anyone, as mentioned above) because they will all leave you happy, and you'll feel free and open, and like the world is yours for the taking. You'll feel absolutely great.
-Why the fuck would you ever be a prude? I mean seriously, what the fuck? You're denying yourself a whole world of potential pleasures if you fight your natural urges. That and being shy. Fuck being shy, that shit sucks. You don't enjoy anything if you're shy.
-Worship Satan. Make him happy. Satan is the only one who will give you pleasure, and God will give you nothing but pain and misery. "But," you ask, "how do you worship Satan when you insist that the story of God is a crock of shit?" Well that's easy. Are you ready for the answer? You ready for it? Really? Well, children, that Satan that you're being told to worship... is you. Yep, you're a god, dude. You = the Satan you're supposed to worship. You're the one that matters most to you. Make you happy.
-The three parts of Satanism are antitheism, because of the direct opposition against Christianity, humanism, because of the belief that the individual is the most important in existence, and a touch of religious fervor, because without zeal, you wouldn't have the motivation to improve yourself to be awesome. Think about it, people promise to work out and quit smoking and junk on their own terms, but they never go through with it because they only have intellectual motivation. But once you accept Satanism intellectually, you can freely "lose yourself" in Satanism in a way that can only be described as "religiously." You think crusaders would do what they did historically if it weren't for religious motivation? Well, use that same religious motivation to improve yourself so that you're more able to enjoy life.
-The majority of the world is Satanist without knowing it. All they're missing is the recognition that Christianity is stupid.
-Satanism encourages "evil" acts. Why does it encourage "evil" acts? Well, the Church, being ever so popular among the masses of idiots, defined most of the terminology used by people. So when they saw Satanism rear its glorious head, they feared it, and quickly unleashed propaganda about it calling everything they believe to be "evil." So if stuff we do, like make lots of friends and have fun with little to no inconvenience to people around us, is recognized as "evil" by the vast majority, we'll call it that, too, so it's easier to identify.

Why is it called Satanism, though? Why couldn't it be called something nicer?
For three reasons, as I see it:
-Satanism idealizes the (fictional) Christian anti-hero, Lucifer, known later as Satan. This (fictional) character is the epitome of the fight against oppression and restriction (God, another character in the same fictional story). Satan was totally fuckin' hot, and he revelled in it (ain't no reason to cover up your hotness). Satan didn't feel like being oppressed by that "God" asshole, so he fought for his freedom. And even though this isn't part of Satanic beliefs, it's my personal belief that he won that battle against God, because he got the better side of human nature, while God was stuck with the shithole that was abstinence, repression, and subjugation, basically anything that kills your self-esteem and keeps it down.
-A title like "Satanism" shows its clear opposition to Christianity.
-Satanism wouldn't be noticed if it weren't for the shock value. The name has done much more to attract the attention, the curiosity, and ultimately converts to Satanism.

That's about it. If you really want more on doctrine, read the first two books (and first two books only) of the Satanic Bible. If you read more than the first two books, I can guarantee you that you'll be thrown off by the content of the second books. If you do ever decide to read them, drop me a line so I can explain to you what you're going to see and why you shouldn't laugh your ass off and lose your respect for Satanism.

...how about if I try to put down the philosophy that has made me the master of my social life ever since I discovered it a few years ago? That should do it.
Here's the idea:
Think of any situation that would cause any slight amount of panic or hesitation for you. It could be things like a friend of yours saying something strange or out of the ordinary, a girl you don't normally talk to flirting with you, things like that. The point is that there are so goddamned many of them. Then consider how much of your overall ability to do just about anything in those situations is limited by your hesitation, if not obliterated outright. Your thinking, judgment, and capacity for rational thought is shot completely. This is the initial realization, and it's pretty important. It's much deeper than I can say in words... meditate on it or something. And no, if you think you have it, you don't. Keep thinking. I'm serious.
Once you've accepted this and understood how huge of an impact it has in any social situation, there are two things you should do. Both take plenty of practice and focus, but they're most definitely worth it:
-Don't give in to hesitation. You can call it a zen thing if you want, but it really involves refusing to think. Panic and hesitation only comes when you fully grasp how odd and/or huge the situation is and how fucked you are if you don't do things perfectly, and the limitations to your ability to act and your mental faculties are only an issue because your mind considers the situation so important that it won't let the fact that you're totally frickin' screwed escape your head, and because that's all that remains in your mind, you don't have much room to do anything else. It's hard to explain, but you'll get what I mean if you just reread this a few times slowly, if you didn't on the first go. When something surprises you, force your mouth to open and say something, and refuse to think about the situation. It's very hard, and I guarantee you you'll get some pretty retarded results when you first try, but it's worth it in the end. For example, someone who doesn't refuse to assess the situation would stare at an attractive girl that just came up to talk to him with his jaw slack, his mind only on the idea "omfg this girl is totally fucking talking to me, I can't screw this up!" But someone who does refuse to assess the situation will force his mind to think nothing of it and act without hesitation. Once you eliminate hesitation, you remove by far the largest drawback anyone can have in a social situation.
-Use hesitation to your advantage impulsively. Another great way to put it (which I only found randomly a few weeks ago) is a quote that is somehow tied to Darth Maul - "Fear is my ally." This is extremely tricky to master (I hope to get that good some day). When you talk to people, be direct and keep them on their toes all the time. Represented physically, it'd be you pushing the person you're talking to backwards to the point where they have to move back or fall on their ass. Be courteous, of course, with plenty of intense eye contact. I found that 100% of the time (and I've been conducting this social experiment for a little over two years now), your friends will revere you and immediately consider you the best leader and wisest thinker they know, your enemies (if you're nice) will become laughably submissive, and girls will more often than not subconsciously confuse their panic and hesitation (all induced by you) for romantic feelings, and so you get some added fun there, too. But what is it you can do to keep people hesitating like this? Well, it's very easy if you have an active and powerful imagination (but especially if you have acting skills), though you're fucked if you don't. The reason this is so hard is because you have to follow multiple thought patterns in any given conversation. Not only are you thinking about your side of the conversation, but you're also thinking what the other person is thinking and analyzing what kinds of things you could say to knock them off balance, while still making sure nothing knocks you off balance. The one general strategy I can give you is this: make it so that the end of your sentence fills their head with simultaneous thoughts, most of which will induce some kind of panic or hesitation individually.
For example, let's say you're having a casual talk with a friend of yours and out of nowhere he starts lying to you about something. It would be stupid to try to cleverly trap him by proving that parts of his story couldn't work out, because that will make him focus primarily on inventing a story even though it will make him start to panic a little. A better way would be to keep looking straight into his eyes as you pause after hearing his response, then say something like, "alright, I'm going to ask you again, and this time I'd appreciate it if you didn't lie to me. What blah blah blah?" Yes, it does sound like you're interrogating them, but think of it this way - if you were in that position and you told what you thought was a convenient lie and he starts slowly doubting you, you could manage to uphold the story by adding things and leaving things ambiguous, etc.; but if you were asked politely to stop lying, you'd turn red instantly, and you'll receive all sorts of lingering and panic-inducing thoughts like, "oh shit, he knows I'm lying," "for future reference, it's a bad idea to lie to this guy," "I don't want him to get angry at me," "I hope he's not angry right now," and "I wasn't very nice to lie to him, was I?" All sorts of these will strike all at the same time, and the first words out of your mouth if you're hit with that rush of panic will be something like "uhhh... um.... uh..." And think about it - you'd consider that person influential and intelligent, and it'd feel more like you have to earn his respect (which people constantly strive for).

Anyway, if you don't get the idea, then either you don't deserve to know it or I'm bad at explaining these things.
Wow, that ended up about 17 times bigger than I intended it to be.

And I'm bisexual, by the way. 2 of those 7 girls I mentioned above are actually guys.

Cheerio!

I could have sworn I answered this a while ago, only with a slightly more detailed question...
Quote: "what is the answer to life @ the uNiverse, and N?", 2005-06-29T21:01:24Z
For this one I can give you many different answers. I'll let you decide which is the best.
1). 42.
2). Beat the game and you'll find out.
3). Cognito ergo sum (I think therefore I am). We exist because we think. If we were not concious, then we might exist, but we would not be aware of it. Imagine if you had no senses at all. You would not be aware of a 'you' because your whole universe would be just 'you', so there would be nothing to compare 'you' with. The purpose of life, then is to feel.
4). Whipped cream
5). There is no answer. If humanity did not exist, then the universe as a whole would continue as normal. Life is pretty pointless and insignificant, really, in comparison with the awesome majesty of the stars.
6). The answer is, prehaps, a more interesting question.
7). There is a theory that should anyone discover the answer to life, the universe and everythig, then the whole universe would instantly vanish and be replaced by something even more bizzare and inexplicable.
There is another theory that states that this has allready happened.
8). We are in the matrix, we are slaves, there is no spoon. *vanishes in a puff of logic*
9). We are in a computer game. As games get better and better, and more immersive, the whole of humanity eventually goes into it's game, and forgets about life in the real world. Then the cycle restarts, as in-game games get better and better...
10). Me. That's right, me. I am the purpose for which this universe was made, this planet is merely a rock made for me to stand on.

#83166 196 2005-07-13T10:15:54Z iangb
Quote
..But whyyyyyy? :huh:
I'm sorry. Excreta Occurs. To often, in my opinion.

Quote: maximo, 2005-07-13T10:02:03Z
why does the saying "there is a first time for everything" exist when something like this:

Quote
You being ransacked by a moroding horde of blood thirsty pink unicorns hell bent on bringing an end to violence in the world via the use of excessive fear to scare the mean out of everyone... all wearing skirts with white lacing and their names handstiched on my a naked midget clown wearing clothes for the first time in his life.

isn't ever gonna have a first time?
In an infinite universe, anything that is physically possible can - and will - happen. If you flip coins for eternity, you are guarenteed (sp?) to get 1000 heads in a row at some point - in fact you should get that outcome an infinitely large number of times. The human mind is very bad at encompasing just what exactly infinity is. We tend to think of it as just a very very big number, forgetting that and number can have 1 added to it. So if the universe goes on for infinity, and the situation you described is physically possible, then it will happen for the first time. And probably for many times after that. I don't know how physically possible bloodthirsty pink unicorns are, though.

And evolution, like any scientific theory, can be disproven if there is enough evidence against it. The trouble is that there isn't any evidence against it (that I know of - and I think I missed out on the first Evolution debate). Much like the beliefs of an atheist, or a Catholic, can be disproven - but we are unlikely to get any evidence for either side. The only thing we can do is to interpret the facts we do have in our own ways - leading to differing beliefs across the world.

Try Amazon.com or something for TP books, allthough you don't really need to read them in order - you'll just get a few more of the in-jokes.

The UK A-level system is basically each subject gets a grade of A-F, based on %score in modular tests. An A is 80%, B is 70%, etc. Universitys most often ask for grades, allthough some ask for percenages. So an AAB means I need over 80% in two subjects and over 70% in one (out of four I'm doing, allthough one is super-hard further maths).

The idea behind it is this: religion can have very strong control over people, and the purpose of religion (the goal in mind of the guy who invented the concept) is to show people, by gradually making these "ultimately correct" religions seem more and more ridiculous with each passing year, that they should pursue truth and think for themselves.

Pascal's wager: If the atheist is wrong about the Bible, then he goes to hell. If the Christian is wrong, then all he has done is tried to live his life by guiding principles that, if everone followed, we would have world peace.

#137588 500 2006-01-30T23:00:04Z BSBen
Ok, suki.

How do you get a .sol file to save somewhere other than where it is supposed to be without it being corrupted? I need to back up my files.
#137679 501 2006-01-31T02:43:47Z iangb
Quote: BSBen, 2006-01-30T23:00:04Z
How do you get a .sol file to save somewhere other than where it is supposed to be without it being corrupted? I need to back up my files.
Just:
Play N.
Go into the folder containing the .sol file (C:\Documents and Settings\[your user name]\Application Data\Macromedia\Flash Player\#SharedObjects\[random letters/numbers]\localhost)
Copy the file n_v14b_userdata.sol to somewhere else.

Done!

To restore your data, simply copy the .sol file back into the main folder.

Hah, you're getting the whole 'awkward silence' thing. Yeah, that happens, and I've gone through it a couple of times(under slightly different circumstances). The only thing to do is: be patient. Keep on being friendly, don't give up.

I think (note: my opinion only) that people have a kind of 'mental image' about everyone they know, and that image can take a while to change. For example, I allways used to be the shortest out of a group of people and I still kept the 'midget' nickname for a while after I was taller than most of them. But over time that image of me as 'the short kid' was replaced by the image of 'me as I am now, the immensly cool guy([/sarc])'. I think you get a certain type of behaviour associated with the mental image - "that's how I react towards him", basically. And that, too, takes a while to change. But it will.

As for your wierd girl... I don't know her, so I can't say exactly what to do. But I would reccomend reviewing your own 'mental picture' of her. She's probably not as bad as you think she is.

#189660 574 2006-06-15T00:30:34Z Keron Cyst
How is even 3.14 achieved? How can pi be calculated in the first place??
#189664 575 2006-06-15T00:39:20Z iangb
Well... actually, I may have lied with the 'to infinity' bit.

Originally, Pi was defined as C=2(Pi)R, where C = circumference of a circle and R=radius of the circle. From that simple equation, Pi=C/2R: so pi can be calculated by measuring the diameter and circumference of a circle very, very accurately and dividing one by the other. In that case, your precision of pi is limited by the accuracy of the measurements of the circle.

However, pi crops up unexpectedly in many places (for example, e^(Pi*i)=-1, which is very, very strange), so it wouldnt suprise me if there was an 'exact' value for pi.

#227382 617 2006-09-11T23:14:18Z iangb
Apologies to Fol67 for not seeing this sooner. I blame the new password, myself.
So...

Ask her what she thinks of her boyfriend, and what he does, and what she wants to do in the future. If she leaves him out of her own choice, not seeming like you have told her to, then things will go much smoother.
If he dumps her, or things get... complicated, just be there for her. Be someone to talk to, a shoulder to cry on, a friend who won't judge her or take advantage of her while she is confused. If she asks for help, give it, but don't force your aid onto her unless she genuinely looks suicidal/self-harming/that sort of thing. And if that does happen, tell her parents (I'm assuming they aren't one source of her problems). When she needs it, the more help she gets, the better.

#258228 642 2006-11-22T19:12:16Z brocerius
Suki, i have a question.

Isnt there a philiosophy or a religion (i cant remember which) that belives something like this; If you belive your actions to be good then they are good.

If so, would Hitler be a 'good' person because he belived what he was doing was for the betterment of humanity? Or does the fact that he presumably realised that torture is not 'good' detract from this? Basically im wandering whether, if this is indeed a religion, could Hitler be in heaven?
#258298 643 2006-11-22T22:35:23Z iangb
There is a mindset - not so much a religion - of moral relitivism, which is often misunderstood. It isn't so much that if you believe you are doing good then you are good, more that there is no ultimate 'good' or 'evil', but different viewpoints on the same action/motive. So Hitler was 'good', but only from his point of view. The rest of us are free to disagree with him.

As to the religious aspects... I've never heard of something like that, allthough it's entirely possible, given what sometimes passes as religion. Moral reletivism fails if God exists, because God's point of view is by definition allways the correct one. So if Hitler is in heaven, it is because God agrees with him. Given the steriotypical Christian fundamentalist view of the (God-murdering, money-stealing) Jews, that might be the case, but I doubt it.

EDIT: Oh, and there was only chaos because i was on holiday for a while. That was years ago, though.

#260291 652 2006-11-27T04:03:17Z iangb
Quote: Twistkill, 2006-11-27T01:55:33Z
Condoms don't protect men from STDs. They only prevent women from getting pregnant, and even that's not guaranteed.
O RLY? http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Condom#In_preventing_STDs
Quote
According to a 2001 report by the National Institutes of Health,[15] correct and consistent use of latex condoms:

reduces the risk of HIV/AIDS transmission by approximately 85% relative to risk when unprotected. See overall HIV transmission rates.
reduces the risk of gonorrhea for men by approximately 71% relative to risk when unprotected.
A University of Washington study published in the New England Journal of Medicine in June 2006 reports that proper condom use decreases the risk of transmission for human papilloma virus by approximately 70%.[16]

Contraceptive Technology concluded that condom failure due to breakage and leakage amounted to 8.08 percent per sexual encounter. [citation needed]

Other studies have shown that the proper and consistent use of condoms prevented HIV from spreading from an infected partner to a non-infected partner in every case.

While different studies show a wide range of results, every scientific study verifies that engaging in sex with a STD positive partner without a condom increases the chances of transmitting an STD when compared to sex with a condom.
You ain't half wrong.

#304749 704 2007-03-12T21:30:04Z spect
What is the direction of electrical current? I'm especially interested in the direction used in common electronics, like when you're working with Kirchhoff's circuit laws.

For what I know, the actual direction is from minus to plus, but my father claims that usually, they agree that the direction is from plus to minus.
#304811 705 2007-03-13T00:11:21Z Atilla
Well, I'm not iangb, but...

Conventional current runs from positive to negative, and this definition is used for most circuit laws and suchlike. However, this flow of current is actually caused by negative charge moving towards the positive terminal in metal wires and suchlike. So, the current flows from positive to negative, but the particles carrying it don't necessarily do so.
#304837 706 2007-03-13T01:33:51Z iangb
On current... there are two types. 'Conventional' current and 'actual' current. 'Actual' current is the flow of electrons, which move from negative to positive, in accordance with electromagnetism. However, 'conventional' current is the opposite - this moves from positive to negative, and is the one which is generally used (for example in a circuit diagram a diode 'points' in the direction of conventional current). The best way to describe conventional current is... well, see below.

Here is a ASCII version of a wire, changing over time. A '-' is an electron, a '_' is plain wire, (no electron), and (-)/(+) are positive/negative terminals.

(-)-----_(+)
(-)----_-(+)
(-)---_--(+)
(-)--_---(+)
(-)-_----(+)
(-)_-----(+)

You can see that as time passes, the electrons are all attracted to - and move towards - the positive terminal. However, the 'gap' (the '_') moves towards the negative terminal. This is the source of (or, if not that, at least a good way of thinking about) 'conventional' current.

If you want an easier way to remember it, just remember that 'conventional' goes in the opposite direction to 'actual'.

Majikthise - a while ago, your posts were reduced due to massive amounts of blatant ressurection/spam that you were commiting. Have a gander at your warn log. The red text is automatic for your post count.

I assume your sig was offensive in some way, but as I don't know what it was like originally I can't comment.

EDIT: whoops, my ASCII wire was infected with smilies. Fixed.
#304838 707 2007-03-13T01:40:12Z Atilla
Quote: iangb, March 13, 2007 11:33 am
'Actual' current is the flow of electrons, which move from negative to positive, in accordance with electromagnetism.

That's not always true. In some systems, current is carried by positive ions. In most things you'll deal with (ie metal wires) it's electrons, though.
#304842 708 2007-03-13T01:48:19Z iangb
...touche.

I was talking about a general case, though. Liquid ion transfer is generally not used as part of an electric circuit, more in electrolysis.

#305842 711 2007-03-17T14:13:09Z iangb
It's harder (or occasionally easier, depending on what you're doing) to make money from strangers as opposed to friends, allthough it makes it much easier to collect if you do it for a charity - the only problem with doing it for charity is you need a permit, which can be got through talking to the appropriate people on town councils. If it's not for charity... I don't know the legal side of it.

I'd say, if you're confident that you can do it safely and it's something you're interested in, go for it. At the moment I collect for charity in random cities on the occasional weekend (with my uni's RAG, mainly dressed as a giant kangaroo), and it's been awesome. London might not be the best place to start though, unless it's close to you. I know they react well to charity, but I dunno about busking. I have it on good authority that Edinburugh is a good place, allthough that is charity-related again.

#327188 723 2007-06-04T00:52:56Z iangb
tricky choice...

The easy option is to let it all play out and see what happens without 'intefering' any more than you normally would. That way you don't risk misinterpreting anyone's intentions towards you, but you don't close down anything either. But if everyone did that, then no-one would ever get anywhere.

I can't really give you advice on this in terms of choice. My best advice would be: be yourself, go for those goals which you think will make you happy, don't be afraid to talk to people, try not to fool yourself into thinking something is more than it actually is, don't worry if you do.

#328380 728 2007-06-07T11:33:53Z iangb
*looks at maps*

Because they're all bland and show no real style?

Your DDAs all overuse jump pads (with the exception of your second one) and none of them contain an element of danger such as a homing missile. Close calls = good, unless you're making something incredibly technical (like my one here [/shameless plug])

Your puzzles are old and uninspired - especially your latest one, which is an ancient glitch which has been used countless times - mainly better. A good puzzle map should force the player to be smart (like Valves), to be precise (like Psychotic Mined Games) or to know the game well (like Formica's Bugged series).

Your action maps are all blocky and spam objects - try using more than just full and 45 degree tiles, and DON'T SPAM OBJECTS. Also, play the map through a couple of times before you submit it to see if you actually enjoy playing it. There are a bunch of action and general tutorials, like M&R's Instructible or jeffkillian's mapmaking guide.

Your N-Arts are meh, but I don't make them so I can't advise you on it. It definitely takes talent, though - if you don't have it, you should try too hard to get it.

Quote: Twistkill, 2007-07-13T04:28:58Z
http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Fusion_power

Now, I'm really interested to understand what the hell they're talking about, but I don't understand it because I'm an uneducated little human. All I get out of it is that we can get a whole lot of energy from two nuclei being fused together, and this fusion reactor creates heat 10 times hotter than the core of the sun. Is there any way you can explain this in simpler terms?
Uh... only sort of. I can say the basic ideas again, in simpler words, though.

Nuclear fusion is, essentially, when two seperate atomic nuclei combine to form a single, larger, atomic nucleus. The physics behind that bit is deceptively simple - there are two important forces at work; electromagnetism and the 'strong nuclear force'. Because a nucleus is made up of protons (+ve charge) and neutrons (no charge), every nucleus has a positive electromagnetic charge. This means that over almost every distance, two nuclei will repel each other (like repels like). However, the 'strong nuclear force' is a much stronger attractive force, but it only works across a much shorter range. So if you can get two nuclei close enough to each other, the strong nuclear force will overcome the electromagnetic force and the two nucleii will stick together.
If you can get them to stick together, they tend to briefly form an unstable heavy nucleus, which then (in laymans terms) 'explodes' into two seperate, stable bits - generally a heavy nucleus and a single neutron. Because these components are more 'stable' (sorry, laymans terms again) than the starting nuclei, a lot of energy is produced in the 'explosion'.
At the moment, the only way to get a fusion reaction to take place is with immense heat and pressure - fusion reactions occur in the sun (which is noted for it's high temperature) and inside the blast from a hydrogen bomb - the first reaction is set off by a seperate method, and then the explosion feeds itself (energy from the first reaction sparks the nexts, and so on). However, experiments are being carried out to try and use a controlled form of fusion as an energy source - using superheated plasma (charged gas) suspended in a magnetic field as fuel. So far, it's been unsuccesfull, but theres a large test reactor being built that might just work. We'll see.

Tough love is the best option here, unless your friend is taking his depression to extremes. If he tells you how he cried for two hours, be sympathetic, but ask him what good it did. If he complains he's never felt worse, tell him to do something about it. If the inevitable 'like what' comes up, suggest he actually has fun with his friends rather than moaning to them - he might find if he takes his mind off his problems for a while he'll realise quite how daft he's been.
If he keeps perstering you, complain to him that he keeps pestering you. Not viciously, but dropping a line like 'nothings going to change if you just keep whining about it' might help matters. You just need to remind him that theres more to life than one girl.

The monocle fad was a fad started in IRC. Lord_day and Clifty were debating about it to a point where it became an all out war in there. Then I witnessed people starting to wear anti/pro monocle avvies. I set up a topic questioning the reason of this monocle business. Once that question sparked, It became the world's shortest forum war with a total of 8 hrs. I was the one who sparked the whole war. There were no maps involved, just avatars. And for the record... anti-monocle won. The thread with the question was eliminated by Tsukatu, but the inquiry in T&I is still there. And to this day, I'm still pro monocle.

Long live the monocle! p_o
